dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNote:\u003c/strong\u003e iFlow, Qwen Code and Gemini CLI free tiers were discontinued in 2026. Use Kiro / OpenCode Free / Vertex instead.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKiro AI\u003c/strong\u003e moved to a paid model in Sep 2025 — the free tier is now capped at \u003cstrong\u003e50 credits/month\u003c/strong\u003e (plus 500 trial credits for new accounts in the first 30 days). Paid tiers: Pro $20/mo (1,000 credits), Pro+ $40/mo (2,000), Pro Max $100/mo (5,000), Power $200/mo (10,000).\n\u003cstrong\u003eOpenCode Free\u003c/strong\u003e model list fluctuates over time (some models free only for limited promos) — subject to change without notice.\n\u003cstrong\u003eVertex AI\u003c/strong\u003e: the $300 free credit for new GCP accounts is still valid, but since Mar 2026 the \u003cstrong\u003eGemini API endpoint no longer consumes these credits\u003c/strong\u003e — call the \u003cstrong\u003eVertex AI Studio\u003c/strong\u003e endpoint instead.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🔑 API Key Providers dir=\"auto\"\u003e🏠 Self-hosted Providers\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--self-hosted-providers\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🏠 Self-hosted Providers\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor speech and embeddings served from \u003cstrong\u003eyour own\u003c/strong\u003e machine — whisper.cpp,\nfaster-whisper, Speaches, Kokoro-FastAPI, openedai-speech, llama.cpp/llama-server,\nvLLM, Infinity, text-embeddings-inference, or anything else that speaks the OpenAI\nshape.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eProvider\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eEndpoint used\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eTypical server\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSelf-hosted STT\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/v1/audio/transcriptions\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ewhisper.cpp, faster-whisper\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSelf-hosted TTS\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/v1/audio/speech\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKokoro-FastAPI, openedai-speech\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSelf-hosted Embedding\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/v1/embeddings\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ellama-server, vLLM, Infinity\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEvery other speech provider is a named cloud service with a fixed endpoint. These\nthree read their address from \u003cstrong\u003eeach connection\u003c/strong\u003e, so one provider can front\nseveral machines and load-balance across them like any other.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSet it on the connection as \u003ccode\u003eproviderSpecificData.baseUrl\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eProvider\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eGive it\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eResult\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSelf-hosted STT\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ethe full URL — \u003ccode\u003ehttp://host:8080/v1/audio/transcriptions\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eused as-is\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSelf-hosted TTS\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ethe server root — \u003ccode\u003ehttp://host:8880\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e+ /v1/audio/speech\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSelf-hosted Embedding\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ethe \u003cstrong\u003eOpenAI base\u003c/strong\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e/v1\u003c/code\u003e included — \u003ccode\u003ehttp://host:8080/v1\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e+ /embeddings\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMind the \u003ccode\u003e/v1\u003c/code\u003e on embeddings.\u003c/strong\u003e The adapter appends \u003ccode\u003e/embeddings\u003c/code\u003e, so\n\u003ccode\u003ehttp://host:8080\u003c/code\u003e resolves to \u003ccode\u003ehttp://host:8080/embeddings\u003c/code\u003e and misses the\nOpenAI route — llama-server answers \u003cstrong\u003e501\u003c/strong\u003e. Give it the same base URL an OpenAI\nclient would use. A full \u003ccode\u003e.../v1/embeddings\u003c/code\u003e is also accepted, so a value pasted\nfrom a \u003ccode\u003ecurl\u003c/code\u003e example works too.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe API key is not checked by most local servers, but the field must be non-empty:\nit is what gives the connection a credentials record, and \u003ccode\u003ebaseUrl\u003c/code\u003e lives there.\nAny placeholder works.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSelf-hosted Embedding has \u003cstrong\u003eno cloud fallback by design\u003c/strong\u003e — a connection saved\nwithout a \u003ccode\u003ebaseUrl\u003c/code\u003e is reported as a configuration error rather than quietly\nfalling back to \u003ccode\u003eapi.openai.com\u003c/code\u003e, which would send your input text and API key to\na third party under a provider named \"Self-hosted\".\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e💡 Key Features\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--key-features\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eFeature\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWhat It Does\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWhy It Matters\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🚀 \u003cstrong\u003eRTK Token Saver\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk\"\u003eRTK\u003c/a\u003e ⭐40K)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCompress tool outputs (\u003ccode\u003egit diff\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003egrep\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003els\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etree\u003c/code\u003e...) before sending to LLM\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSave \u003cstrong\u003e20-40% input tokens\u003c/strong\u003e per request\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🧠 \u003cstrong\u003eHeadroom Token Saver\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/chopratejas/headroom\"\u003eHeadroom\u003c/a\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOptional external \u003ccode\u003e/v1/compress\u003c/code\u003e proxy before provider routing\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSave more context tokens without changing clients\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🪨 \u003cstrong\u003eCaveman Mode\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman\"\u003eCaveman\u003c/a\u003e ⭐52K)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInject caveman-speak prompt → LLM replies terse, technical substance preserved\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSave \u003cstrong\u003eup to 65% output tokens\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🐴 \u003cstrong\u003ePonytail\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail\"\u003ePonytail\u003c/a\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInject \"lazy senior dev\" prompt → LLM writes minimal, YAGNI-first code (Lite/Full/Ultra)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFewer output tokens, less refactoring\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🎯 \u003cstrong\u003eSmart 3-Tier Fallback\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAuto-route: Subscription → Cheap → Free\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNever stop coding, zero downtime\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e📊 \u003cstrong\u003eReal-Time Quota Tracking\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLive token count + reset countdown\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMaximize subscription value\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🔄 \u003cstrong\u003eFormat Translation\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOpenAI ↔ Claude ↔ Gemini ↔ Cursor ↔ Kiro ↔ Vertex\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWorks with any CLI tool\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e👥 \u003cstrong\u003eMulti-Account Support\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMultiple accounts per provider\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLoad balancing + redundancy\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🔄 \u003cstrong\u003eAuto Token Refresh\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOAuth tokens refresh automatically\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo manual re-login needed\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🎨 \u003cstrong\u003eCustom Combos\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCreate unlimited model combinations\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTailor fallback to your needs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e📝 \u003cstrong\u003eRequest Logging\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDebug mode with full request/response 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTool outputs (\u003ccode\u003egit diff\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003egrep\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003efind\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003els\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etree\u003c/code\u003e, log dumps...) often eat 30-50% of your prompt budget. RTK detects them and applies smart, lossless compression \u003cstrong\u003ebefore\u003c/strong\u003e the request hits the LLM:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFilters:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003egit-diff\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003egit-status\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003egrep\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003efind\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003els\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etree\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ededup-log\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003esmart-truncate\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eread-numbered\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003esearch-list\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAuto-detect:\u003c/strong\u003e No config needed — RTK peeks the first 1KB of each \u003ccode\u003etool_result\u003c/code\u003e and picks the right filter.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSafe by design:\u003c/strong\u003e If a filter fails, throws, or makes output bigger, RTK silently keeps the original text. Errors never break your request.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUniversal:\u003c/strong\u003e Works across all formats (OpenAI, Claude, Gemini, Cursor, Kiro, OpenAI Responses) because it runs \u003cstrong\u003ebefore\u003c/strong\u003e any format translation.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDefault ON:\u003c/strong\u003e Toggle anytime in Dashboard → Endpoint settings.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Without RTK: 47K tokens sent to LLM\nWith RTK:    28K tokens sent to LLM   (40% saved · same context · same answer)\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eWithout RTK: 47K tokens sent to LLM\nWith RTK:    28K tokens sent to LLM   (40% saved · same context · same answer)\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" \u003ccode\u003e/v1/compress\u003c/code\u003e endpoint, then keeps normal routing, fallback, auth, and usage tracking:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\nheadroom proxy --port 8787\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEnable in Dashboard → Endpoint → Token Saver → Headroom. Default URL: \u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost:8787\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDocker examples:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell network\u003c/span\u003e\nhttp://headroom:8787\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Headroom running on host request.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🐴 Ponytail (Lazy Senior Dev)\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--ponytail-lazy-senior-dev\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🐴 Ponytail (Lazy Senior Dev)\" href=\"#-ponytail-lazy-senior-dev\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePonytail injects a \u003cem\u003e\"lazy senior dev\"\u003c/em\u003e system prompt into every request, biasing the LLM toward minimal, YAGNI-first code — deletion over addition, stdlib over new deps, one-liners over abstractions. Adapted from \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail\"\u003eDietrichGebert/ponytail\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLite\u003c/strong\u003e — Build what's asked, name the lazier alternative.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFull\u003c/strong\u003e — YAGNI ladder enforced: stdlib → native → existing deps → one-liner → minimal code.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUltra\u003c/strong\u003e — YAGNI extremist: deletion first, ship the one-liner, challenge the rest of the requirement in the same response.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Without Ponytail: verbose code, extra abstractions, \u0026quot;just in case\u0026quot; scaffolding\nWith Ponytail:    shortest working diff, no unrequested abstractions, fewer tokens\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eWithout Ponytail: verbose code, extra abstractions, \"just in case\" scaffolding\nWith Ponytail:    shortest working diff, no unrequested abstractions, fewer tokens\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNever trades away: input validation, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, or anything explicitly requested. Enable in Dashboard → Endpoint → Ponytail. Stacks with Caveman (output terseness) and RTK (input compression).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" (cloud sync endpoint base)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eNEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eNEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL\u003c/code\u003e are still supported for compatibility/UI, but server runtime now prioritizes \u003ccode\u003eBASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003eCLOUD_URL\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCloud sync requests now use timeout + fail-fast behavior to avoid UI hanging when cloud DNS/network is unavailable.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e📊 Usage dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \"cost\" displayed in Usage Analytics is \u003cstrong\u003efor tracking and comparison purposes only\u003c/strong\u003e.\n9Router itself \u003cstrong\u003enever charges\u003c/strong\u003e you anything. You only pay providers directly (if using paid services).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExample:\u003c/strong\u003e If your dashboard shows \"$290 total cost\" while using Kiro free models, this represents\nwhat you would have paid using paid APIs directly. Your actual cost = \u003cstrong\u003e$0\u003c/strong\u003e (Kiro free tier: ~50 credits/mo).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThink of it as a \"savings tracker\" showing how much you're saving by using free models or\nrouting through 9Router!\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" Why does my dashboard show high costs?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe dashboard tracks your token usage and displays \u003cstrong\u003eestimated costs\u003c/strong\u003e as if you were using paid APIs directly. This is \u003cstrong\u003enot actual billing\u003c/strong\u003e - it's a reference to show how much you're saving by using free models or existing subscriptions through 9Router.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExample:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDashboard shows:\u003c/strong\u003e \"$290 total cost\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReality:\u003c/strong\u003e You're using Kiro free models (~50 credits/mo)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYour actual cost:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003cstrong\u003e$0.00\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhat $290 means:\u003c/strong\u003e Amount you \u003cstrong\u003esaved\u003c/strong\u003e by using free models instead of paid APIs!\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe cost display is a \"savings tracker\" to help you understand your usage patterns and optimization opportunities.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003e💳 Will I be charged by 9Router?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNo.\u003c/strong\u003e 9Router is free, open-source software that runs on your own computer. It never charges you anything.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYou only pay:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ \u003cstrong\u003eSubscription providers\u003c/strong\u003e (Claude Code $20/mo, Codex $20-200/mo) → Pay them directly on their websites\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ \u003cstrong\u003eCheap providers\u003c/strong\u003e (GLM, MiniMax) → Pay them directly, 9Router just routes your requests\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e❌ \u003cstrong\u003e9Router itself\u003c/strong\u003e → \u003cstrong\u003eNever charges anything, ever\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e9Router is a local proxy/router. It doesn't have your credit card, can't send invoices, and has no billing system. It's completely free software.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003e🆓 Are FREE providers really unlimited?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMostly!\u003c/strong\u003e The current FREE providers (Kiro, OpenCode Free, Vertex) are genuinely free, but free tiers have limits:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThese are free services offered by those respective companies:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKiro AI\u003c/strong\u003e: ~50 credits/month free (plus 500 trial credits for new accounts in the first 30 days) via AWS Builder ID / Google / GitHub OAuth. Paid tiers available above that.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOpenCode Free\u003c/strong\u003e: No-auth passthrough proxy, models auto-fetched from \u003ccode\u003eopencode.ai/zen/v1/models\u003c/code\u003e. The free model list fluctuates over time (some models free only for limited promos) — subject to change without notice.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eVertex AI\u003c/strong\u003e: $300 free credits for new Google Cloud accounts (90 days). Since Mar 2026 the Gemini API endpoint no longer consumes these credits — use the \u003cstrong\u003eVertex AI Studio\u003c/strong\u003e endpoint instead.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e9Router just routes your requests to them - there's no \"catch\" or future billing from 9Router itself. They're truly free services, and 9Router makes them easy to use with fallback support.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDiscontinued free tiers (no longer recommended):\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e❌ \u003cstrong\u003eiFlow\u003c/strong\u003e: Was free unlimited, now changed to paid (2026)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e❌ \u003cstrong\u003eQwen Code\u003c/strong\u003e: Free OAuth tier fully discontinued by Alibaba on 2026-04-15\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e❌ \u003cstrong\u003eGemini CLI\u003c/strong\u003e: Service fully shut down by Google on 2026-06-18 (replaced by the closed-source Antigravity CLI). Discontinued — do not use.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003e💰 How do I minimize my actual AI costs?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFree-First Strategy:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStart with 100% free combo:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"1. kr/glm-5 (GLM-5 free via Kiro, ~50 credits/mo)\n2. OpenCode Free models (no auth, auto-fetched)\n3. Vertex AI Gemini 3 Pro (using the Vertex AI Studio endpoint with $300 credits)\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e1. kr/glm-5 (GLM-5 free via Kiro, ~50 credits/mo)\n2. OpenCode Free models (no auth, auto-fetched)\n3. Vertex AI Gemini 3 Pro (using the Vertex AI Studio endpoint with $300 credits)\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCost: $0/month\u003c/strong\u003e (within Kiro's free credit cap; OpenCode/Vertex subject to their free-tier limits)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAdd cheap backup\u003c/strong\u003e only if you need it:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"4. glm/glm-4.7 ($0.6/1M tokens)\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e4. glm/glm-4.7 ($0.6/1M tokens)\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAdditional cost: Only pay for what you actually use\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse subscription providers last:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOnly if you already have them\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e9Router helps maximize their value through quota tracking\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eResult:\u003c/strong\u003e Most users can operate at $0/month using only free tiers!\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003e\u003cb\u003e📈 What if my usage suddenly spikes?\u003c/b\u003e\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e9Router's smart fallback prevents surprise charges:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eScenario:\u003c/strong\u003e You're on a coding sprint and blow through your quotas\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWithout 9Router:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e❌ Hit rate limit → Work stops → Frustration\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e❌ Or: Accidentally rack up huge API bills\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWith 9Router:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ Subscription hits limit → Auto-fallback to cheap tier\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ Cheap tier gets expensive → Auto-fallback to free tier\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ Never stop coding → Predictable costs\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYou're in control:\u003c/strong\u003e Set spending limits per provider in dashboard, and 9Router respects them.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" login password when no saved hash app data location (SQLite at \u003ccode\u003e$DATA_DIR/db/data.sqlite\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePORT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eframework default\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eService port (\u003ccode\u003e20128\u003c/code\u003e in examples)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eHOSTNAME\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eframework default\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBind host (Docker defaults to \u003ccode\u003e0.0.0.0\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eNODE_ENV\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eruntime default\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSet \u003ccode\u003eproduction\u003c/code\u003e for deploy\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eBASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost:20128\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eServer-side internal base URL used by cloud sync jobs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eCLOUD_URL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehttps://9router.com\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eServer-side cloud sync endpoint base URL\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eNEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost:3000\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBackward-compatible/public base URL (prefer \u003ccode\u003eBASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e for server runtime)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eNEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehttps://9router.com\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBackward-compatible/public cloud URL (prefer \u003ccode\u003eCLOUD_URL\u003c/code\u003e for server runtime)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAPI_KEY_SECRET\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eendpoint-proxy-api-key-secret\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHMAC secret for generated API keys\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eMACHINE_ID_SALT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eendpoint-proxy-salt\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSalt for stable machine ID hashing\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003efalse\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEnables request/response logs under \u003ccode\u003elogs/\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAUTH_COOKIE_SECURE\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003efalse\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eForce \u003ccode\u003eSecure\u003c/code\u003e auth cookie (set \u003ccode\u003etrue\u003c/code\u003e behind HTTPS reverse proxy)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eREQUIRE_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003efalse\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEnforce Bearer API key on \u003ccode\u003e/v1/*\u003c/code\u003e routes (recommended for internet-exposed deploys)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eHTTP_PROXY\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eHTTPS_PROXY\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eALL_PROXY\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eNO_PROXY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eempty\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOptional outbound proxy for upstream provider calls\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSEARXNG_URL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost:8888/search\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEndpoint for the built-in unauthenticated SearXNG web-search provider\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNotes:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLowercase proxy variables are also supported: \u003ccode\u003ehttp_proxy\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ehttps_proxy\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eall_proxy\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eno_proxy\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e is not baked into Docker image (\u003ccode\u003e.dockerignore\u003c/code\u003e); inject runtime config with \u003ccode\u003e--env-file\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e-e\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOn Windows, \u003ccode\u003eAPPDATA\u003c/code\u003e can be used for local storage path resolution.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eINSTANCE_NAME\u003c/code\u003e appears in older docs/env templates, but is currently not used at <p dir="auto"><strong>Never stop coding. Save 20-40% tokens with RTK + auto-fallback to FREE &amp; cheap AI models.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Connect All AI Code Tools (Claude Code, Cursor, Antigravity, Copilot, Codex, Gemini, OpenCode, Cline, OpenClaw...) to 40+ AI Providers &amp; 100+ Models.</strong></p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤔 Why 9Router?</h2><a id="user-content--why-9router" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤔 Why 9Router?" href="#-why-9router"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Stop wasting money, tokens and hitting limits:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>❌ Subscription quota expires unused every month</li>
<li>❌ Rate limits stop you mid-coding</li>
<li>❌ Tool outputs (git diff, grep, ls...) burn tokens fast</li>
<li>❌ Expensive APIs ($20-50/month per provider)</li>
<li>❌ Manual switching between providers</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>9Router solves this:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>✅ <strong>RTK Token Saver</strong> - Auto-compress tool_result content, save 20-40% tokens per request</li>
<li>✅ <strong>Maximize subscriptions</strong> - Track quota, use every bit before reset</li>
<li>✅ <strong>Auto fallback</strong> - Subscription → Cheap → Free, zero downtime</li>
<li>✅ <strong>Multi-account</strong> - Round-robin between accounts per provider</li>
<li>✅ <strong>Universal</strong> - Works with Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Cline, any CLI tool</li>
</ul>
<hr>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">⚡ Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content--quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ⚡ Quick Start" href="#-quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>1. Install globally:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install -g 9router
9router"><pre>npm install -g 9router
9router</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">🎉 Dashboard opens at <code>http://localhost:20128</code></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>2. Connect a FREE provider (no signup needed):</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">Dashboard → Providers → Connect <strong>Kiro AI</strong> (~50 credits/month free: Claude 4.5 + GLM-5 + MiniMax) or <strong>OpenCode Free</strong> (no auth) → Done!</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>3. Use in your CLI tool:</strong></p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Claude Code/Codex/OpenClaw/Cursor/Cline Settings:
  Endpoint: http://localhost:20128/v1
  API Key: [copy from dashboard]
  Model: kr/claude-sonnet-4.5"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Claude Code/Codex/OpenClaw/Cursor/Cline Settings:
  Endpoint: http://localhost:20128/v1
  API Key: [copy from dashboard]
  Model: kr/claude-sonnet-4.5
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>That's it!</strong> Start coding with FREE AI models.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Alternative: run from source (this repository):</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">This repository package is private (<code>9router-app</code>), so source/Docker execution is the expected local development path.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cp .env.example .env
npm install
PORT=20128 NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128 npm run dev"><pre>cp .env.example .env
npm install
PORT=20128 NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128 npm run dev</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Production mode:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm run build
PORT=20128 HOSTNAME=0.0.0.0 NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128 npm run start"><pre>npm run build
PORT=20128 HOSTNAME=0.0.0.0 NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128 npm run start</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Default URLs:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Dashboard: <code>http://localhost:20128/dashboard</code></li>
<li>OpenAI-compatible API: <code>http://localhost:20128/v1</code></li>
</ul>
<hr>

<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> iFlow, Qwen Code and Gemini CLI free tiers were discontinued in 2026. Use Kiro / OpenCode Free / Vertex instead.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Kiro AI</strong> moved to a paid model in Sep 2025 — the free tier is now capped at <strong>50 credits/month</strong> (plus 500 trial credits for new accounts in the first 30 days). Paid tiers: Pro $20/mo (1,000 credits), Pro+ $40/mo (2,000), Pro Max $100/mo (5,000), Power $200/mo (10,000).
<strong>OpenCode Free</strong> model list fluctuates over time (some models free only for limited promos) — subject to change without notice.
<strong>Vertex AI</strong>: the $300 free credit for new GCP accounts is still valid, but since Mar 2026 the <strong>Gemini API endpoint no longer consumes these credits</strong> — call the <strong>Vertex AI Studio</strong> endpoint instead.</p>
</blockquote>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🏠 Self-hosted Providers</h3><a id="user-content--self-hosted-providers" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🏠 Self-hosted Providers" href="#-self-hosted-providers"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For speech and embeddings served from <strong>your own</strong> machine — whisper.cpp,
faster-whisper, Speaches, Kokoro-FastAPI, openedai-speech, llama.cpp/llama-server,
vLLM, Infinity, text-embeddings-inference, or anything else that speaks the OpenAI
shape.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Provider</th>
<th>Endpoint used</th>
<th>Typical server</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Self-hosted STT</strong></td>
<td><code>/v1/audio/transcriptions</code></td>
<td>whisper.cpp, faster-whisper</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Self-hosted TTS</strong></td>
<td><code>/v1/audio/speech</code></td>
<td>Kokoro-FastAPI, openedai-speech</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Self-hosted Embedding</strong></td>
<td><code>/v1/embeddings</code></td>
<td>llama-server, vLLM, Infinity</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Every other speech provider is a named cloud service with a fixed endpoint. These
three read their address from <strong>each connection</strong>, so one provider can front
several machines and load-balance across them like any other.</p>
<p dir="auto">Set it on the connection as <code>providerSpecificData.baseUrl</code>:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Provider</th>
<th>Give it</th>
<th>Result</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Self-hosted STT</td>
<td>the full URL — <code>http://host:8080/v1/audio/transcriptions</code></td>
<td>used as-is</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Self-hosted TTS</td>
<td>the server root — <code>http://host:8880</code></td>
<td><code>+ /v1/audio/speech</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Self-hosted Embedding</td>
<td>the <strong>OpenAI base</strong>, <code>/v1</code> included — <code>http://host:8080/v1</code></td>
<td><code>+ /embeddings</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Mind the <code>/v1</code> on embeddings.</strong> The adapter appends <code>/embeddings</code>, so
<code>http://host:8080</code> resolves to <code>http://host:8080/embeddings</code> and misses the
OpenAI route — llama-server answers <strong>501</strong>. Give it the same base URL an OpenAI
client would use. A full <code>.../v1/embeddings</code> is also accepted, so a value pasted
from a <code>curl</code> example works too.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">The API key is not checked by most local servers, but the field must be non-empty:
it is what gives the connection a credentials record, and <code>baseUrl</code> lives there.
Any placeholder works.</p>
<p dir="auto">Self-hosted Embedding has <strong>no cloud fallback by design</strong> — a connection saved
without a <code>baseUrl</code> is reported as a configuration error rather than quietly
falling back to <code>api.openai.com</code>, which would send your input text and API key to
a third party under a provider named "Self-hosted".</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">💡 Key Features</h2><a id="user-content--key-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 💡 Key Features" href="#-key-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Feature</th>
<th>What It Does</th>
<th>Why It Matters</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>🚀 <strong>RTK Token Saver</strong> (<a href="https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk">RTK</a> ⭐40K)</td>
<td>Compress tool outputs (<code>git diff</code>, <code>grep</code>, <code>ls</code>, <code>tree</code>...) before sending to LLM</td>
<td>Save <strong>20-40% input tokens</strong> per request</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🧠 <strong>Headroom Token Saver</strong> (<a href="https://github.com/chopratejas/headroom">Headroom</a>)</td>
<td>Optional external <code>/v1/compress</code> proxy before provider routing</td>
<td>Save more context tokens without changing clients</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🪨 <strong>Caveman Mode</strong> (<a href="https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman">Caveman</a> ⭐52K)</td>
<td>Inject caveman-speak prompt → LLM replies terse, technical substance preserved</td>
<td>Save <strong>up to 65% output tokens</strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🐴 <strong>Ponytail</strong> (<a href="https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail">Ponytail</a>)</td>
<td>Inject "lazy senior dev" prompt → LLM writes minimal, YAGNI-first code (Lite/Full/Ultra)</td>
<td><strong>Fewer output tokens, less refactoring</strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🎯 <strong>Smart 3-Tier Fallback</strong></td>
<td>Auto-route: Subscription → Cheap → Free</td>
<td>Never stop coding, zero downtime</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>📊 <strong>Real-Time Quota Tracking</strong></td>
<td>Live token count + reset countdown</td>
<td>Maximize subscription value</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🔄 <strong>Format Translation</strong></td>
<td>OpenAI ↔ Claude ↔ Gemini ↔ Cursor ↔ Kiro ↔ Vertex</td>
<td>Works with any CLI tool</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>👥 <strong>Multi-Account Support</strong></td>
<td>Multiple accounts per provider</td>
<td>Load balancing + redundancy</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🔄 <strong>Auto Token Refresh</strong></td>
<td>OAuth tokens refresh automatically</td>
<td>No manual re-login needed</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🎨 <strong>Custom Combos</strong></td>
<td>Create unlimited model combinations</td>
<td>Tailor fallback to your needs</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>📝 <strong>Request Logging</strong></td>
<td>Debug mode with full request/response logs</td>
<td>Troubleshoot issues easily</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>💾 <strong>Cloud Sync</strong></td>
<td>Sync config across devices</td>
<td>Same setup everywhere</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>📊 <strong>Usage Analytics</strong></td>
<td>Track tokens, cost, trends over time</td>
<td>Optimize spending</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🌐 <strong>Deploy Anywhere</strong></td>
<td>Localhost, VPS, Docker, Cloudflare Workers</td>
<td>Flexible deployment options</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Set <code>X-9Router-Token-Saver: off</code> to bypass all token savers for one chat request.</p>
<details>
<summary><b>📖 Feature Details</b></summary>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 RTK Token Saver</h3><a id="user-content--rtk-token-saver" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 RTK Token Saver" href="#-rtk-token-saver"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Tool outputs (<code>git diff</code>, <code>grep</code>, <code>find</code>, <code>ls</code>, <code>tree</code>, log dumps...) often eat 30-50% of your prompt budget. RTK detects them and applies smart, lossless compression <strong>before</strong> the request hits the LLM:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Filters:</strong> <code>git-diff</code>, <code>git-status</code>, <code>grep</code>, <code>find</code>, <code>ls</code>, <code>tree</code>, <code>dedup-log</code>, <code>smart-truncate</code>, <code>read-numbered</code>, <code>search-list</code></li>
<li><strong>Auto-detect:</strong> No config needed — RTK peeks the first 1KB of each <code>tool_result</code> and picks the right filter.</li>
<li><strong>Safe by design:</strong> If a filter fails, throws, or makes output bigger, RTK silently keeps the original text. Errors never break your request.</li>
<li><strong>Universal:</strong> Works across all formats (OpenAI, Claude, Gemini, Cursor, Kiro, OpenAI Responses) because it runs <strong>before</strong> any format translation.</li>
<li><strong>Default ON:</strong> Toggle anytime in Dashboard → Endpoint settings.</li>
</ul>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Without RTK: 47K tokens sent to LLM
With RTK:    28K tokens sent to LLM   (40% saved · same context · same answer)"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Without RTK: 47K tokens sent to LLM
With RTK:    28K tokens sent to LLM   (40% saved · same context · same answer)
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🧠 Headroom Token Saver</h3><a id="user-content--headroom-token-saver" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🧠 Headroom Token Saver" href="#-headroom-token-saver"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Headroom is optional and runs separately. 9Router calls Headroom's local <code>/v1/compress</code> endpoint, then keeps normal routing, fallback, auth, and usage tracking:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Client → 9Router → Headroom /v1/compress → 9Router → provider"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Client → 9Router → Headroom /v1/compress → 9Router → provider
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Local setup:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install &quot;headroom-ai[proxy]&quot;
headroom proxy --port 8787"><pre>pip install <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>headroom-ai[proxy]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
headroom proxy --port 8787</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Enable in Dashboard → Endpoint → Token Saver → Headroom. Default URL: <code>http://localhost:8787</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Docker examples:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Headroom service in same Docker network
http://headroom:8787

# Headroom running on host machine
http://host.docker.internal:8787"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Headroom service in same Docker network</span>
http://headroom:8787

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Headroom running on host machine</span>
http://host.docker.internal:8787</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If Headroom is down or returns an error, 9Router fails open and sends the original request.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🐴 Ponytail (Lazy Senior Dev)</h3><a id="user-content--ponytail-lazy-senior-dev" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🐴 Ponytail (Lazy Senior Dev)" href="#-ponytail-lazy-senior-dev"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Ponytail injects a <em>"lazy senior dev"</em> system prompt into every request, biasing the LLM toward minimal, YAGNI-first code — deletion over addition, stdlib over new deps, one-liners over abstractions. Adapted from <a href="https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail">DietrichGebert/ponytail</a>.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Lite</strong> — Build what's asked, name the lazier alternative.</li>
<li><strong>Full</strong> — YAGNI ladder enforced: stdlib → native → existing deps → one-liner → minimal code.</li>
<li><strong>Ultra</strong> — YAGNI extremist: deletion first, ship the one-liner, challenge the rest of the requirement in the same response.</li>
</ul>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Without Ponytail: verbose code, extra abstractions, &quot;just in case&quot; scaffolding
With Ponytail:    shortest working diff, no unrequested abstractions, fewer tokens"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Without Ponytail: verbose code, extra abstractions, "just in case" scaffolding
With Ponytail:    shortest working diff, no unrequested abstractions, fewer tokens
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Never trades away: input validation, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, or anything explicitly requested. Enable in Dashboard → Endpoint → Ponytail. Stacks with Caveman (output terseness) and RTK (input compression).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🎯 Smart 3-Tier Fallback</h3><a id="user-content--smart-3-tier-fallback" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🎯 Smart 3-Tier Fallback" href="#-smart-3-tier-fallback"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔄 Format Translation</h3><a id="user-content--format-translation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔄 Format Translation" href="#-format-translation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Seamless translation between formats:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>OpenAI</strong> ↔ <strong>Claude</strong> ↔ <strong>Gemini</strong> ↔ <strong>Cursor</strong> ↔ <strong>Kiro</strong> ↔ <strong>Vertex</strong> ↔ <strong>Antigravity</strong> ↔ <strong>Ollama</strong> ↔ <strong>OpenAI Responses</strong></li>
<li>Your CLI tool sends OpenAI format → 9Router translates → Provider receives native format</li>
<li>Works with any tool that supports custom OpenAI endpoints</li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Cloud Runtime Notes</h4><a id="user-content-cloud-runtime-notes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Cloud Runtime Notes" href="#cloud-runtime-notes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Prefer server-side cloud variables in production:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>BASE_URL</code> (internal callback URL used by sync scheduler)</li>
<li><code>CLOUD_URL</code> (cloud sync endpoint base)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><code>NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL</code> and <code>NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL</code> are still supported for compatibility/UI, but server runtime now prioritizes <code>BASE_URL</code>/<code>CLOUD_URL</code>.</li>
<li>Cloud sync requests now use timeout + fail-fast behavior to avoid UI hanging when cloud DNS/network is unavailable.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📊 Usage Analytics</h3><a id="user-content--usage-analytics" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📊 Usage Analytics" href="#-usage-analytics"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Track token usage per provider and model</li>
<li>Cost estimation and spending trends</li>
<li>Monthly reports and insights</li>
<li>Optimize your AI spending</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>💡 IMPORTANT - Understanding Dashboard Costs:</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">The "cost" displayed in Usage Analytics is <strong>for tracking and comparison purposes only</strong>.
9Router itself <strong>never charges</strong> you anything. You only pay providers directly (if using paid services).</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Example:</strong> If your dashboard shows "$290 total cost" while using Kiro free models, this represents
what you would have paid using paid APIs directly. Your actual cost = <strong>$0</strong> (Kiro free tier: ~50 credits/mo).</p>
<p dir="auto">Think of it as a "savings tracker" showing how much you're saving by using free models or
routing through 9Router!</p>
</blockquote>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📊 Understanding 9Router Costs &amp; Billing</h3><a id="user-content--understanding-9router-costs--billing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📊 Understanding 9Router Costs &amp; Billing" href="#-understanding-9router-costs--billing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>9Router Billing Reality:</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">✅ <strong>9Router software = FREE forever</strong> (open source, never charges)<br>
✅ <strong>Dashboard "costs" = Display/tracking only</strong> (not actual bills)<br>
✅ <strong>You pay providers directly</strong> (subscriptions or API fees)<br>
✅ <strong>FREE providers stay FREE</strong> (Kiro ~50 credits/mo, OpenCode Free, Vertex $300 credits = $0 within free-tier limits) — note iFlow/Qwen/Gemini CLI free tiers were discontinued in 2026
❌ <strong>9Router never sends invoices</strong> or charges your card</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>How Cost Display Works:</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">The dashboard shows <strong>estimated costs</strong> as if you were using paid APIs directly. This is <strong>not billing</strong> - it's a comparison tool to show your savings.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Example Scenario:</strong></p>

<details>
<summary><b>📊 Why does my dashboard show high costs?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">The dashboard tracks your token usage and displays <strong>estimated costs</strong> as if you were using paid APIs directly. This is <strong>not actual billing</strong> - it's a reference to show how much you're saving by using free models or existing subscriptions through 9Router.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Example:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Dashboard shows:</strong> "$290 total cost"</li>
<li><strong>Reality:</strong> You're using Kiro free models (~50 credits/mo)</li>
<li><strong>Your actual cost:</strong> <strong>$0.00</strong></li>
<li><strong>What $290 means:</strong> Amount you <strong>saved</strong> by using free models instead of paid APIs!</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The cost display is a "savings tracker" to help you understand your usage patterns and optimization opportunities.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>💳 Will I be charged by 9Router?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto"><strong>No.</strong> 9Router is free, open-source software that runs on your own computer. It never charges you anything.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>You only pay:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>✅ <strong>Subscription providers</strong> (Claude Code $20/mo, Codex $20-200/mo) → Pay them directly on their websites</li>
<li>✅ <strong>Cheap providers</strong> (GLM, MiniMax) → Pay them directly, 9Router just routes your requests</li>
<li>❌ <strong>9Router itself</strong> → <strong>Never charges anything, ever</strong></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">9Router is a local proxy/router. It doesn't have your credit card, can't send invoices, and has no billing system. It's completely free software.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>🆓 Are FREE providers really unlimited?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Mostly!</strong> The current FREE providers (Kiro, OpenCode Free, Vertex) are genuinely free, but free tiers have limits:</p>
<p dir="auto">These are free services offered by those respective companies:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Kiro AI</strong>: ~50 credits/month free (plus 500 trial credits for new accounts in the first 30 days) via AWS Builder ID / Google / GitHub OAuth. Paid tiers available above that.</li>
<li><strong>OpenCode Free</strong>: No-auth passthrough proxy, models auto-fetched from <code>opencode.ai/zen/v1/models</code>. The free model list fluctuates over time (some models free only for limited promos) — subject to change without notice.</li>
<li><strong>Vertex AI</strong>: $300 free credits for new Google Cloud accounts (90 days). Since Mar 2026 the Gemini API endpoint no longer consumes these credits — use the <strong>Vertex AI Studio</strong> endpoint instead.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">9Router just routes your requests to them - there's no "catch" or future billing from 9Router itself. They're truly free services, and 9Router makes them easy to use with fallback support.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Discontinued free tiers (no longer recommended):</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>❌ <strong>iFlow</strong>: Was free unlimited, now changed to paid (2026)</li>
<li>❌ <strong>Qwen Code</strong>: Free OAuth tier fully discontinued by Alibaba on 2026-04-15</li>
<li>❌ <strong>Gemini CLI</strong>: Service fully shut down by Google on 2026-06-18 (replaced by the closed-source Antigravity CLI). Discontinued — do not use.</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>💰 How do I minimize my actual AI costs?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Free-First Strategy:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Start with 100% free combo:</strong></p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="1. kr/glm-5 (GLM-5 free via Kiro, ~50 credits/mo)
2. OpenCode Free models (no auth, auto-fetched)
3. Vertex AI Gemini 3 Pro (using the Vertex AI Studio endpoint with $300 credits)"><pre class="notranslate"><code>1. kr/glm-5 (GLM-5 free via Kiro, ~50 credits/mo)
2. OpenCode Free models (no auth, auto-fetched)
3. Vertex AI Gemini 3 Pro (using the Vertex AI Studio endpoint with $300 credits)
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Cost: $0/month</strong> (within Kiro's free credit cap; OpenCode/Vertex subject to their free-tier limits)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Add cheap backup</strong> only if you need it:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="4. glm/glm-4.7 ($0.6/1M tokens)"><pre class="notranslate"><code>4. glm/glm-4.7 ($0.6/1M tokens)
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Additional cost: Only pay for what you actually use</strong></p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Use subscription providers last:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Only if you already have them</li>
<li>9Router helps maximize their value through quota tracking</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Result:</strong> Most users can operate at $0/month using only free tiers!</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary><b>📈 What if my usage suddenly spikes?</b></summary>
<p dir="auto">9Router's smart fallback prevents surprise charges:</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Scenario:</strong> You're on a coding sprint and blow through your quotas</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Without 9Router:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>❌ Hit rate limit → Work stops → Frustration</li>
<li>❌ Or: Accidentally rack up huge API bills</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>With 9Router:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>✅ Subscription hits limit → Auto-fallback to cheap tier</li>
<li>✅ Cheap tier gets expensive → Auto-fallback to free tier</li>
<li>✅ Never stop coding → Predictable costs</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>You're in control:</strong> Set spending limits per provider in dashboard, and 9Router respects them.</p>

}</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">OpenClaw</h3><a id="user-content-openclaw" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OpenClaw" href="#openclaw"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Option 1 — Dashboard (recommended):</strong></p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Dashboard → CLI Tools → OpenClaw → Select Model → Apply"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Dashboard → CLI Tools → OpenClaw → Select Model → Apply
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Option 2 — Manual:</strong> Edit <code>~/.openclaw/openclaw.json</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;agents&quot;: {
    &quot;defaults&quot;: {
      &quot;model&quot;: {
        &quot;primary&quot;: &quot;9router/kr/claude-sonnet-4.5&quot;
      }
    }
  },
  &quot;models&quot;: {
    &quot;providers&quot;: {
      &quot;9router&quot;: {
        &quot;baseUrl&quot;: &quot;http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1&quot;,
        &quot;apiKey&quot;: &quot;sk_9router&quot;,
        &quot;api&quot;: &quot;openai-completions&quot;,
        &quot;models&quot;: [
          {
            &quot;id&quot;: &quot;kr/claude-sonnet-4.5&quot;,
            &quot;name&quot;: &quot;Claude Sonnet 4.5 (Kiro Free)&quot;
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"agents"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"defaults"</span>: {
      <span class="pl-ent">"model"</span>: {
        <span class="pl-ent">"primary"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>9router/kr/claude-sonnet-4.5<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
      }
    }
  },
  <span class="pl-ent">"models"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"providers"</span>: {
      <span class="pl-ent">"9router"</span>: {
        <span class="pl-ent">"baseUrl"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
        <span class="pl-ent">"apiKey"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>sk_9router<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
        <span class="pl-ent">"api"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>openai-completions<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
        <span class="pl-ent">"models"</span>: [
          {
            <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>kr/claude-sonnet-4.5<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
            <span class="pl-ent">"name"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Claude Sonnet 4.5 (Kiro Free)<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }
}</pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> OpenClaw only works with local 9Router. Use <code>127.0.0.1</code> instead of <code>localhost</code> to avoid IPv6 resolution issues.</p>
</blockquote>
