<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">VPS Deployment</h3><a id="user-content-vps-deployment" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: VPS Deployment" href="#vps-deployment"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Clone and install
git clone https://github.com/decolua/9router.git
cd 9router
npm install
npm run build

# Configure
export JWT_SECRET=&quot;your-secure-secret-change-this&quot;
export INITIAL_PASSWORD=&quot;your-password&quot;
export DATA_DIR=&quot;/var/lib/9router&quot;
export PORT=&quot;20128&quot;
export HOSTNAME=&quot;0.0.0.0&quot;
export NODE_ENV=&quot;production&quot;
export NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=&quot;http://localhost:20128&quot;
export NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL=&quot;https://9router.com&quot;
export API_KEY_SECRET=&quot;endpoint-proxy-api-key-secret&quot;
export MACHINE_ID_SALT=&quot;endpoint-proxy-salt&quot;

# Start
npm run start

# Or use PM2
npm install -g pm2
pm2 start npm --name 9router -- start
pm2 save
pm2 startup"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Clone and install</span>
git clone https://github.com/decolua/9router.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> 9router
npm install
npm run build

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Configure</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> JWT_SECRET=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>your-secure-secret-change-this<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> INITIAL_PASSWORD=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>your-password<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> DATA_DIR=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>/var/lib/9router<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> PORT=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>20128<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> HOSTNAME=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>0.0.0.0<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> NODE_ENV=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>production<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>http://localhost:20128<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>https://9router.com<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> API_KEY_SECRET=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>endpoint-proxy-api-key-secret<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> MACHINE_ID_SALT=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>endpoint-proxy-salt<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Start</span>
npm run start

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or use PM2</span>
npm install -g pm2
pm2 start npm --name 9router -- start
pm2 save
pm2 startup</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Docker</h3><a id="user-content-docker" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Docker" href="#docker"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Published images (multi-platform <code>linux/amd64</code> + <code>linux/arm64</code>):</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Docker Hub: <a href="https://hub.docker.com/r/decolua/9router" rel="nofollow"><code>decolua/9router</code></a></li>
<li>GHCR: <a href="https://github.com/decolua/9router/pkgs/container/9router"><code>ghcr.io/decolua/9router</code></a></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Quick start (use published image):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -d \
  --name 9router \
  -p 20128:20128 \
  -v &quot;$HOME/.9router:/app/data&quot; \
  -e DATA_DIR=/app/data \
  decolua/9router:latest"><pre>docker run -d \
  --name 9router \
  -p 20128:20128 \
  -v <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$HOME</span>/.9router:/app/data<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
  -e DATA_DIR=/app/data \
  decolua/9router:latest</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">→ Open <a href="http://localhost:20128" rel="nofollow">http://localhost:20128</a></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Build from source (dev):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/decolua/9router.git
cd 9router/app
docker build -t 9router .
docker run -d --name 9router -p 20128:20128 \
  -v &quot;$HOME/.9router:/app/data&quot; -e DATA_DIR=/app/data 9router"><pre>git clone https://github.com/decolua/9router.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> 9router/app
docker build -t 9router <span class="pl-c1">.</span>
docker run -d --name 9router -p 20128:20128 \
  -v <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$HOME</span>/.9router:/app/data<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> -e DATA_DIR=/app/data 9router</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Container defaults:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>PORT=20128</code></li>
<li><code>HOSTNAME=0.0.0.0</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Useful commands:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker logs -f 9router
docker restart 9router
docker stop 9router &amp;&amp; docker rm 9router
docker pull decolua/9router:latest   # update to latest"><pre>docker logs -f 9router
docker restart 9router
docker stop 9router <span class="pl-k">&amp;&amp;</span> docker rm 9router
docker pull decolua/9router:latest   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> update to latest</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Data persistence:</strong> <code>$HOME/.9router/db/data.sqlite</code> on host ↔ <code>/app/data/db/data.sqlite</code> in container.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Environment Variables</h3><a id="user-content-environment-variables" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Environment Variables" href="#environment-variables"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Variable</th>
<th>Default</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>JWT_SECRET</code></td>
<td>Auto-generated (<code>~/.9router/jwt-secret</code>)</td>
<td>JWT signing secret for dashboard auth cookie (override to share across instances)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>INITIAL_PASSWORD</code></td>
<td><code>123456</code></td>
<td>First login password when no saved hash exists</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>DATA_DIR</code></td>
<td><code>~/.9router</code></td>
<td>Main app data location (SQLite at <code>$DATA_DIR/db/data.sqlite</code>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>PORT</code></td>
<td>framework default</td>
<td>Service port (<code>20128</code> in examples)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>HOSTNAME</code></td>
<td>framework default</td>
<td>Bind host (Docker defaults to <code>0.0.0.0</code>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>NODE_ENV</code></td>
<td>runtime default</td>
<td>Set <code>production</code> for deploy</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>BASE_URL</code></td>
<td><code>http://localhost:20128</code></td>
<td>Server-side internal base URL used by cloud sync jobs</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>CLOUD_URL</code></td>
<td><code>https://9router.com</code></td>
<td>Server-side cloud sync endpoint base URL</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL</code></td>
<td><code>http://localhost:3000</code></td>
<td>Backward-compatible/public base URL (prefer <code>BASE_URL</code> for server runtime)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL</code></td>
<td><code>https://9router.com</code></td>
<td>Backward-compatible/public cloud URL (prefer <code>CLOUD_URL</code> for server runtime)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>API_KEY_SECRET</code></td>
<td><code>endpoint-proxy-api-key-secret</code></td>
<td>HMAC secret for generated API keys</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>MACHINE_ID_SALT</code></td>
<td><code>endpoint-proxy-salt</code></td>
<td>Salt for stable machine ID hashing</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS</code></td>
<td><code>false</code></td>
<td>Enables request/response logs under <code>logs/</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE</code></td>
<td><code>false</code></td>
<td>Force <code>Secure</code> auth cookie (set <code>true</code> behind HTTPS reverse proxy)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>REQUIRE_API_KEY</code></td>
<td><code>false</code></td>
<td>Enforce Bearer API key on <code>/v1/*</code> routes (recommended for internet-exposed deploys)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>HTTP_PROXY</code>, <code>HTTPS_PROXY</code>, <code>ALL_PROXY</code>, <code>NO_PROXY</code></td>
<td>empty</td>
<td>Optional outbound proxy for upstream provider calls</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>SEARXNG_URL</code></td>
<td><code>http://localhost:8888/search</code></td>
<td>Endpoint for the built-in unauthenticated SearXNG web-search provider</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Notes:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Lowercase proxy variables are also supported: <code>http_proxy</code>, <code>https_proxy</code>, <code>all_proxy</code>, <code>no_proxy</code>.</li>
<li><code>.env</code> is not baked into Docker image (<code>.dockerignore</code>); inject runtime config with <code>--env-file</code> or <code>-e</code>.</li>
<li>On Windows, <code>APPDATA</code> can be used for local storage path resolution.</li>
<li><code>INSTANCE_NAME</code> appears in older docs/env templates, but is currently not used at runtime.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Runtime Files and Storage</h3><a id="user-content-runtime-files-and-storage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Runtime Files and Storage" href="#runtime-files-and-storage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Main app state: <code>${DATA_DIR}/db/data.sqlite</code> (SQLite — providers, combos, aliases, keys, settings, usage history)</li>
<li>Auto backups: <code>${DATA_DIR}/db/backups/</code></li>
<li>Optional request/translator logs: <code>&lt;repo&gt;/logs/...</code> when <code>ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS=true</code></li>
<li>Both <code>${DATA_DIR}</code> and <code>~/.9router</code> resolve to the same location in a Docker container — the symlink <code>/root/.9router -&gt; /app/data</code> is created at build time.</li>
</ul>
</details>
<hr>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🐛 Troubleshooting</h2><a id="user-content--troubleshooting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🐛 Troubleshooting" href="#-troubleshooting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>"Language model did not provide messages"</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Provider quota exhausted → Check dashboard quota tracker</li>
<li>Solution: Use combo fallback or switch to cheaper tier</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Rate limiting</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Subscription quota out → Fallback to GLM/MiniMax</li>
<li>Add combo: <code>cc/claude-opus-4-7 → glm/glm-5.1 → kr/claude-sonnet-4.5</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>OAuth token expired</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Auto-refreshed by 9Router</li>
<li>If issues persist: Dashboard → Provider → Reconnect</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>High costs</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Enable RTK in Dashboard → Endpoint settings (default ON, saves 20-40% tokens)</li>
<li>Check usage stats in Dashboard</li>
<li>Switch primary model to GLM/MiniMax</li>
<li>Use free tier (Kiro, OpenCode Free, Vertex) for non-critical tasks</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Dashboard opens on wrong port</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Set <code>PORT=20128</code> and <code>NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>First login not working</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Check <code>INITIAL_PASSWORD</code> in <code>.env</code></li>
<li>If unset, fallback password is <code>123456</code></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>No request logs under <code>logs/</code></strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Set <code>ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS=true</code></li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🛠️ Tech Stack</h2><a id="user-content-️-tech-stack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🛠️ Tech Stack" href="#️-tech-stack"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Runtime</strong>: Node.js 20+</li>
<li><strong>Framework</strong>: Next.js 16</li>
<li><strong>UI</strong>: React 19 + Tailwind CSS 4</li>
<li><strong>Database</strong>: SQLite (better-sqlite3 / node:sqlite / sql.js fallback)</li>
<li><strong>Streaming</strong>: Server-Sent Events (SSE)</li>
<li><strong>Auth</strong>: OAuth 2.0 (PKCE) + JWT + API Keys</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📝 API Reference</h2><a id="user-content--api-reference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📝 API Reference" href="#-api-reference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Chat Completions</h3><a id="user-content-chat-completions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Chat Completions" href="#chat-completions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="POST http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions
Authorization: Bearer your-api-key
Content-Type: application/json

{
  &quot;model&quot;: &quot;cc/claude-opus-4-6&quot;,
  &quot;messages&quot;: [
    {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Write a function to...&quot;}
  ],
  &quot;stream&quot;: true
}"><pre>POST http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions
Authorization: Bearer your-api-key
Content-Type: application/json

{
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>model<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>cc/claude-opus-4-6<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>messages<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: [
    {<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>role<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>user<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>content<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Write a function to...<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>}
  ],
  <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>stream<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span>
}</pre></div>

<p dir="auto"><strong><a href="https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute">OmniRoute</a></strong> — A full-featured TypeScript fork of 9Router. Adds 36+ providers, 4-tier auto-fallback, multi-modal APIs (images, embeddings, audio, TTS), circuit breaker, semantic cache, LLM evaluations, and a polished dashboard. 368+ unit tests. Available via npm and Docker.</p>

<p dir="auto">Built on the shoulders of giants:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/router-for-me/CLIProxyAPI">CLIProxyAPI</a></strong> — original Go implementation that inspired this JavaScript port.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/rtk-ai/rtk">RTK</a></strong> <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/336c8986c87fe756cc01e4659b17780c258096dce6e0236b2ce3b7f47f34a4b1/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f73746172732f72746b2d61692f72746b3f7374796c653d666c617426636f6c6f723d79656c6c6f77"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/336c8986c87fe756cc01e4659b17780c258096dce6e0236b2ce3b7f47f34a4b1/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f73746172732f72746b2d61692f72746b3f7374796c653d666c617426636f6c6f723d79656c6c6f77" alt="Stars" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/github/stars/rtk-ai/rtk?style=flat&amp;color=yellow" style="max-width: 100%;"></a> — Rust token-saver. 9Router ports its compression pipeline to JS → <strong>−20-40% input tokens</strong> on every request.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman">Caveman</a></strong> <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/32b08ac2815b5494dc93ae4bc0701891d91bea36607726bcb8707d18a2fd20df/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f73746172732f4a756c697573427275737365652f636176656d616e3f7374796c653d666c617426636f6c6f723d79656c6c6f77"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/32b08ac2815b5494dc93ae4bc0701891d91bea36607726bcb8707d18a2fd20df/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f73746172732f4a756c697573427275737365652f636176656d616e3f7374796c653d666c617426636f6c6f723d79656c6c6f77" alt="Stars" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/github/stars/JuliusBrussee/caveman?style=flat&amp;color=yellow" style="max-width: 100%;"></a> by <strong><a href="https://github.com/JuliusBrussee">@JuliusBrussee</a></strong> — viral <em>"why use many token when few token do trick"</em>. 9Router adapts its prompt → <strong>−65% output tokens</strong>.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail">Ponytail</a></strong> <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/d7f04eb9b918a5881a355529abf4352b11b7b33ba42580760bf8e60e97554883/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f73746172732f44696574726963684765626572742f706f6e797461696c3f7374796c653d666c617426636f6c6f723d79656c6c6f77"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/d7f04eb9b918a5881a355529abf4352b11b7b33ba42580760bf8e60e97554883/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6769746875622f73746172732f44696574726963684765626572742f706f6e797461696c3f7374796c653d666c617426636f6c6f723d79656c6c6f77" alt="Stars" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/github/stars/DietrichGebert/ponytail?style=flat&amp;color=yellow" style="max-width: 100%;"></a> by <strong><a href="https://github.com/DietrichGebert">@DietrichGebert</a></strong> — <em>"lazy senior dev"</em> skill. 9Router injects its YAGNI-first ladder → <strong>fewer tokens, less code, shorter diffs</strong>.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Huge thanks to these authors — without their work, 9Router's token-saving features wouldn't exist. ⭐ them on GitHub!</p>
<hr>

    <div id="js-global-screen-reader-notice-assertive" class="sr-only mt-n1" aria-live="assertive" aria-atomic="true"></div>
  </body>
</html>
